[\[139b.2\]]su spras/_/bya rgod phung po mchog gyur pa/_/gangs kyi ri yi rtse brgya dag_/rab tu 'bar bas mdzes par byas/_/dpal dang ldan pa'i grong khyer 'di/_/lha yi rin chen bla yi tshogs/_/dgra khang dag kyang legs par bcas/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]gzhal med khang mang gis/_/kho 
[\[139b.3\]]ra yug nas mdzes par gyur/_/mtsho bran dang ba rdzing chen mchog_/rdzing bu'i chus ni yul rnams brgyan/_/bder 'jug 'bab stegs rdzing chen mang /_/rgyal po'i khab ni de ring du/_/lha yi grong khyer bzhin du mdzes/_/rnga dang dung dang dril bu dang /_/pi bang rgyud bcas ma sbrengs 
[\[139b.4\]]par/_/sgra chen sgrogs par 'byung ba 'di/_/ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba lags/_/lus bde yid dang 'thad pa yi/_/ser bu'i ngad kyang ldang bar gyur/_/bar snang mkha' las 'brug sgra sgrogs/_/glog mang thams cad rnam par 'gyu/_/rin chen gang ba'i chu rgyun 'bab/_/de dag 
[\[139b.5\]]tshul gyis char 'bab cing /_/sems can mi gnod 'thun par bsil/_/srog chags phra mo sdig sbrul sbrang /_/de ring rab dga' mgu zhing rtse/_/le brgan rtsi dang sa la dang /_/a ti mug ta ti la ka_pun na ka dang ke ta dang /_/ku ru ba ka ke sa ra/_/dri zhim pa dang nag dang /_/
[\[139b.6\]]a ga ru dang dong ka yis/_/'od ma'i tshal ni de ring du/_/shin tu snang zhing mchis par bgyis/_/ba ku la dang tsam pa ka_/thang shing pri ya ku yi shing /_/gseng phrom dang ni nyo ti dang /_/sgron shing dang ni yongs 'du dang /_/shing ljon tshogs kyis 'od ma'i tshal/_/de ring snang zhing 
[\[139b.7\]]mchis par bgyis/_/a mra ta la ka pid tha/_/pa na sa dang tsan dan dmar/_/ba mngal shing ni dri zhim gyis/_/'od ma'i tshal ni deng snang bgyis/_/sra rtsi shing mchog ba rang ga_/ang tsu ka dang ke sa ri/_/mya ngan 'tshang dang tsam pa ka_/dmar ser gyis ni 'od ma'i tshal/_/
[\[140a\]]
[\[140a.1\]]de ring 'bar zhing mchis par bgyis/_/rgya shug 'dzam bu kam pa ri/_/skyu ru bel la se'u shing nags/_/ten du ka dang pa ka sa/_/mngar zhing zhim pa'i shing ljon dang /_/'bra ko'i shing mang rgun shing tshogs/_/bal bu dang ni sa brtol mang /_/ma ni ka ra dong ka dang /_/
[\[140a.2\]]shing ljon tshogs kyis 'od ma'i tshal/_/de ring snang zhing mchis par bgyis/_/a ga ru yi shing dag dang /_/tam la 'dab rab mdzes dang /_/gur kum dang ni phur mo dang /_/sa bon tshogs ni mang po dang /_/gor shi sha dang tsan dan dmar/_/ha ri'i tsan dan du ma dang /_/shing ljon 
[\[140a.3\]]tshogs kyis 'od ma'i tshal/_/de ring snang zhing mchis par bgyis/_/skyed mos tshal dang nags tshal dang /_/rdzing bu mtsho mang dag na yang /_/dus tshigs kun gyi ud pal tshogs/_/me tog rgyas pas 'od ma'i tshal/_/de ring snang zhing mchis par bgyis/_/rin chen kun gyi shing 
[\[140a.4\]]ljon dang /_/rin chen 'khri shing yongs su 'khri/_/rin chen kun gyi ud pal gyi/_/me tog tshogs rnams de ring mdzes/_/sangs rgyas mthus ni de ring du/_/'di ltar nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor dag_/'jig rten khams ni mang por yang /_/dkon pa'i las mang bgyis pa gda'/_/
[\[140a.5\]]'jig rten khams ni mang po dag_/me long gi ni dkyil 'khor ltar/_/sems can bye ba mang po yis/_/'di na mchis bzhin gsal bar mthong /_/ngang pa khrung khrung ngur par bcas/_/shang shang te'u ni mang po dang /_/ka ran da ba rma bya dang /_/de bzhin khu byug ku na la/_/bung ba mtha' 
[\[140a.6\]]yas bung bung sgrogs/_/rgyal po'i khab tu de ring ni/_/bya rnams rnam pa du ma dag_/skad snyan 'byin cing sgrogs par bgyid/_/gnag pa dang ni ljang khu'i mdog_/mdog sdug dang ni sngo ba dang /_/pad ma dkar po'i mdog can gyi/_/ngang mchog glang chen seng ge rnams/_/seng 
[\[140a.7\]]ge'i sgra ni sgrogs bgyid cing /_/rgyal po'i khab ni de ring mdzes/_/'dzam gling yul 'khor bcas pa dang /_/lung par bcas dang tshong rdal bcas/_/'byor rgyas dag la sems can rnams/_/lha yi bde ba'i longs spyod spyad/_/sems dga' mos shing rab tshim nas/_/sems 
[\[140b\]]
[\[140b.1\]]can rnam pa du ma yis/_/las drag mang po'i sdig spangs nas/_/yid bde rab tu dul bar gyur/_/de ring khyod zhi zhabs drung lhags/_/phyag 'tshal nas ni de ring du/_/rnam pa mang po'i tshul gyis 'jug_/nye gnas cho ga mchog la bzod/_/sems can lha mang bye ba rnams/_/
[\[140b.2\]]dam pa'i chos kyi tshig kyang srung /_/dam pa'i chos mchog rab kyi tshul/_/ngang tshul bzang bas nyan par bgyid/_/shin tu mi bzad bdud kyi 'khor/_/de ring rab tu g.yos par gyur/_/dmyal ba'i tshong dus phye nas kyang /_/de ring khyod kyi zhabs drung lhags/_/gzhan yang lha mang bye 
[\[140b.3\]]ba'i tshogs/_/glu gar rnam pa sna tshogs bgyid/_/de bzhin dud 'gror skyes pa yi/_/sems can rnams ni phan tshun du/_/mang du dgra bgyid chags pa'i tshul/_/'khor ba'i tshul spangs chos kyang nyan/_/dmyal ba yi ni sems can rnams/_/sdug bsngal mang po myong ba yang /_/

[\[140b.5\]]mtshar rmad/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshig gcig gsungs na ni/_/ji ltar blo dga' bzhin du sna tshogs rtogs/_/khyod ni sems can kun gyis bde myong ba'i/_/yangs shing rnam dag chos ni ston pa mdzad/_/sems can thams cad yid bde rab dul zhing /_/sems kyang rab dga' 
[\[140b.6\]]mnyam par byams par sems/_/gcig la gcig ni nongs pa bshags nas kyang /_/ma dang bu bzhin chos ni nyan par bgyid/_/sems can kun kyang bdag nyid gtsor bgyis nas/_/de ring bdag gi rang bzhin bsam dgongs snyam/_/bdag la thugs brtses bdag gi stan bzhugs nas/_/

[\[141a\]]
[\[141a.1\]]la bzhugs nas kyang /_/khyod ni chos 'di ston par mdzad/_/lha yi lha la bdag dad nas/_/bdag cag de ring skyabs su mchi/_/khang par gnas pa'i lha kun ni/_/kye ma mi rtag mi brtan no/_/skyabs ma mchis shing mgon ma mchis/_/bsod nams chung zhing sdig par

[\[141a.5\]]lang ba/_/rtag pa'i zla ba yongs su bskor/_/dpe med bskal pa bye mang mdzes/_/khyod kyi yon tan bgrangs las 'das/_/rin chen 'byung gnas de bzhin gshegs/_/dpal chen gzi brjid rtag pa ste/_/bsam yas stobs kyi rgya mtsho lags/_/sems can kun phan bsam mi khyab/_/
[\[141a.6\]]kun la byams shing thugs brtse ba/_/thub sras sgra gcan zin bzhin du/_/sems can kun snyoms byams par dgongs/_/de ring mnyam gyur mnyam par gzigs/_/mthu che stobs kyang che ba lags/_/mi shes srid pa'i mtsho chen las/_/deng khyod bdag ni bsgral du gsol/_/
[\[141a.7\]]bdag cag kun gyis bzod pa gsol/_/de bzhin gshegs pas bzod par mdzod/_/dam chos 'du ston thos nas ni/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba rtogs/_/ma 'tshal ba ni kye re mtshar/_/bdag gis yun ring mi snyan bgyis/_/de bzhin gshegs rtag ma 'tshal ba'i/_/sems 
[\[141b\]]
[\[141b.1\]]can rnams ni ngan 'gror mchi/_/ma 'tshal ba ni kye re mtshar/_/bdag gis yun ring 'khor bar bgyis/_/yongs su mya ngan 'da' snyam mdzad/_/sems can mar me'i nang dag tu/_/phye ma leb ni mchong ba bzhin/_/bcom ldan dam chos de bzhin du/_/yongs su mya ngan 'da' 'gyur

[\[141b.5\]]pa yi bdud rtsi gter/_/bcom ldan sangs rgyas bshad du gsol/_/rnam par sbyong ba'i cho ga yi/_/las kyi tshig ni smos pa rnams/_/bcom ldan sangs rgyas rgyas par mdzod/_/rtag cing brtan pa'i 'du ston la/_/sems can mang po dgod par gsol/_/sprin chen po'i mdo las 
[\[141b.6\]]lha thams cad 'dus pa zhes bya ba ste phung po dang po'i le'u rdzogs so//_//'phags pa sprin chen po'i mdo/_bam po bzhi pa/_de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sprin chen snying pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[141b.7\]]dam pa'i chos brtan pa sprin chen po'i mdo'i tshig smos pa/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa ni a la la ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das a la la sprin chen po'i mdo'i yul bsam gyis mi khyab ste/_cho 'phrul chen po'i rnam par 'phrul 
[\[142a\]]
[\[142a.1\]]pa sems can thams cad kyi mngon sum du gyur pa lags so/_/sprin chen po'i mdo bshad pa na sprin chen po'i tshogs kyis rin po che thams cad dang /_bcud thams cad kyis rgyas ba'i char gyi rgyun gyi tshogs bdud rtsi'i sgra las byung ba rnams kyis bdud rtsi'i char chen po rab tu

[\[142a.6\]]ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i ldem po ngag gi glog ston te/_rtag pa'i sgra'i bcud bsil ba'i de kho na drag cing mi zad pa'i rgyun gyi char gyi tshogs kyis nyon mongs pa'i sbubs kyi lam rab tu sel ba'i dam pa'i chos kyi char chen po rab tu dbab tu gsol/_sems can thams cad mngon sum du de bzhin gshegs pa'i rtag

[\[142b.1\]]bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po legs so legs so/_/'di lta bu'i sprin chen po mngar ba bzhi ro thams cad dang 'dres pa'i char rtag tu 'bebs par 'dod pas ni sprin chen po rdo rje'i mdo gzung bar bya'o/_/gzhan dag la yang rgya cher bshad par bya'o/_/shin tu dga' ba yang bskyed par

[\[142b.5\]]las kyi zhing gi rgya mtsho la sred par yang 'gyur ro/_/tshe rabs thams cad du tha na rmi lam na yang sangs rgyas mthong ba dang 'bral bar yang mi 'gyur ro/_/de dag gi dge ba'i chos rnams la bar chad bya ba'i phyir bdud sdig can glags lta ba dang skabs tshol bas glags rnyed par yang mi 'gyur ro/_/
[\[142b.6\]]nga rgyal choms par yang 'gyur ro/_/gzungs thob par yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ldem po ngag dpyod pa la mkhas par yang 'gyur ro/_/chos thams cad kyi gsang ba'i sgor 'jug par yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gter yang dag par ston par yang 'gyur ro/_/

[\[143a.1\]]pa'i gsung brjod par yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dmigs pa la rnal 'byor par yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyi ma'i 'od zer du yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi gzir yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i

[\[143b.1\]]bar smra bar yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su rab tu byung ba'i sems can rnams la ni tshul khrims nyams pa med do zhes tshul khrims kyi bsngags pa brjod par yang 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa la ni nub par 'gyur ba med do zhes nub par mi 'gyur ba'i bsngags pa

[\[160b.2\]]bo chen po rtag pa'i sku/_/srid skye'i dgyes pa rgya mtsho mnga'/_/gos pa med pa'i de bzhin gshegs/_/'khor ba dag ni dge bar bzhed/_/srid par skye ba'i gleng gzhi'i le'u zhes bya ste lnga pa'o//_//sprin chen snying pos gsol pa/_'khor ba'i nyon mongs pa'i zhing 
[\[160b.3\]]gi las kyi sa thob par bgyi ba'i slad du/_sems kyis yang dag par len pa'i rnam par grol ba'i smon lam gsang ba'i chos kyi sgo bcu bcom ldan 'das kyis rgyas par bshad du gsol/_de bzhin gshegs pas bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'khor 
[\[160b.4\]]ba'i nyon mongs pa'i zhing gi 'bras bu 'thob pa zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba'i nyon mongs pa'i zhing la dga' ba'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba'i sbyor ba la sems gnas pa zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba la mchog tu dga' ba'i gzungs zhes bya ba dang /_'khor bar 'gro ba zhes bya ba dang /_
[\[160b.5\]]'khor bar shin tu char 'bebs pa zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba rgyun mi gcod pa'i rlung gi rgyal po zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba'i chom rkun zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba'i rtsa ba'i spyod yul dri ma med pa zhes bya ba dang /_'khor ba na yun ring du gnas pa rnams la snang bar byed pa zhes bya ste/_bcu po 
[\[160b.6\]]de dag ni 'khor ba'i nyon mongs pa'i zhing gi las kyi sa thob par bya ba'i phyir sems kyis yang dag par len pa'i rnam par grol ba'i smon lam gsang ba'i chos kyi sgo yin no/_/de nas de'i tshe klu'i rgyal po dug med ces bya bas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa byas nas tshigs su bcad pa 'di smras 
[\[160b.7\]]so/_/nyon mongs 'khor ba rnyed pa che/_/sems can rnams la sman slad du/_/'khor ba'i dngos 'dra de bzhin gshegs/_/chos rnams sna tshogs ston par mdzad/_/'khor ba nyon mongs pa'i zhing gi las kyi sa 'thob pa'i gleng gzhi'i le'u zhes bya ste drug pa'o//_//

[\[195a.2\]]phyir yang sems gnas par mi sgom mo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshungs pa med pa'i phyir yang sems gnas par mi sgom mo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i grangs med pa'i phyir yang sems gnas par mi sgom mo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir 
[\[195a.3\]]yang sems gnas par mi sgom mo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan gyi phyir yang sems skyed par mi 'dod do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dpe byad bzang po'i phyir yang sems skyed par mi 'dod do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i gnas kyi phyir yang sems skyed par mi 
[\[195a.4\]]'dod do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dpag tu med pa'i phyir yang sems skyed par mi 'dod do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshungs pa med pa'i phyir yang sems skyed par mi byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i grangs med pa'i phyir yang sems skyed par mi byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs 
[\[195a.5\]]pa'i yon tan dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir yang sems skyed par mi byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang cho 'phrul gyi phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mi 'jigs pa'i phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
[\[195a.6\]]gsang ba'i phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod pa'i gnas bla na med pa'i phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po'i phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung gi sems can thams cad skye ba dang /_rga ba dang /_na ba dang /_'chi ba dang /_
[\[195a.7\]]mya ngan dang /_smre sngags 'don pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_yid mi bde ba dang /_'khrug pa las thar bar 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad kyis bde ba thob par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad grub pa dang bde bar 
[\[195b\]]
[\[195b.1\]]'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad byang chub kyi yon tan la dgod par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad sangs rgyas su bsgrub par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad chos kyi 'khor lo skor du gzhug par 'dod 
[\[195b.2\]]pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad 'phags pa'i dge 'dun yongs su sdud du gzhug par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi gdung mi 'chad par bya bar 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad la chos 
[\[195b.3\]]kyi rgyun mi gcad par bya bar 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad la dge 'dun gyi rigs bsgrub pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems can thams cad la ting nge 'dzin dang /_shes rab dang /_rnam par grol ba bsgrub par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/sems 
[\[195b.4\]]can thams cad kyi tshul khrims ma nyams pa dang /_skyon med pa dang /_ma 'dres pa dang /_ma 'khrugs par 'dod pa'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sprin chen snying pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/'di la gnas pa'i byang 
[\[195b.5\]]chub sems dpa' ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan gyi slad du tshul khrims mi srung ngo zhes bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas ci las dgongs te de skad gsungs/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' la sangs rgyas kyi yon 
[\[195b.6\]]tan bsgrub pa de dag thams cad yod pa'i phyir ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_ting nge 'dzin 'di ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan thams cad kyi 'byung gnas yin pa'i phyir ro/_/sprin chen snying po dper na nor bu rin po che bai dUr+ya du ma dang /_nor thams cad kyi bdag por gyur pa de bdag nyid la yod bzhin du 
[\[195b.7\]]bai dUr+ya'i phyir 'dod pa'i sems dang the tsom skyed par 'gyur ram/_gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bdag nyid la yod bzhin du mtshan gyi phyir 'dod pa'i sems skyed pa de ni gnas med 
[\[196a\]]
[\[196a.1\]]do/_/sprin chen snying po ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i yul dang yon tan thams cad dang ldan par rig par bya'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ci bgyi ba mthar phyin pa dang ldan pa lags par rig par bgyi'am/_bka' 
[\[196a.2\]]stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po de skad ma zer cig_/'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni gang gi tshe sems can thams cad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas par byas pa na bya ba mthar phyin par 'gyur te/_de'i tshe 'di la gnas pa'i 
[\[196a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa'i bya ba thams cad mthar phyin par 'gyur ro/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gang dag ting nge 'dzin 'di'i yul bshad pa thos nas spong bar mi bgyid pa ni kye ma ngo mtshar te/_sems can de dag ni mya ngan du bgyi mi 'tshal ba lags so/_/bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen 
[\[196a.4\]]snying po de de bzhin te/_ma 'ongs pa'i dus na ting nge 'dzin bstan pa 'di bshad pa na gang dag spong bar mi byed pa'i sems can de dag la ni mya ngan bya mi dgos so/_/'di lta ste/_dper na rgyal po'i bu rigs che shing stobs dang ldan la 'khor snying nye ba dang /_tshul du bsrung bar bya ba dang /_yon 
[\[196a.5\]]tan dang ldan zhing rgyal po chen por dbang bskur ba'i spu la bab pa ni gnas dma' ba'i las la 'dod pa'i sems mi skyed de/_rgya chen po'i rgyal srid kyi gnas la sems so/_/de bzhin du ting nge 'dzin bstan pa 'di bshad pa thos na spong bar mi byed cing kha ton du byed pa dag ni chos kyi yon tan thams 
[\[196a.6\]]cad dang ldan zhing byang chub chen por dbang bskur ba'i 'os yin te/_ting nge 'dzin 'di thos nas ting nge 'dzin gyi mchog 'di las gzhan pa gnas dma' ba la 'dod pa'i sems mi skyed de de ni gnas med do/_/ting nge 'dzin rgya chen po'i gnas 'di yang rkyen du bab pa yin no/_/sprin chen snying po 
[\[196a.7\]]gzhan yang dper na gdol pa zol pa ni mchod pa chen pos dbang bskur ba'i 'os ma yin no/_/de bzhin du gdol pa zol pa dang 'dra ba'i sems can dag kyang ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po 'di'i dbang bskur ba'i 'os su mi 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems 
[\[196b\]]
[\[196b.1\]]dpa' rnams ni sangs rgyas mang po la bsnyen bkur byas pa yin par rig par bya ba'i phyir ro/_/sprin chen snying po gzhan yang ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i yul la ltos/_ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni de bzhin gshegs 
[\[196b.2\]]pa'i yon tan rtag pa dang /_ther zug pa dang /_brtan pa dang /_zhi ba bstan par bya ba'i phyir ting nge 'dzin 'di mi shes pa'i sems can rnams la khong du chud par bya'o snyam nas/_sems can gang dang gang la ci dang cis don du 'gyur ba de dang de dag la de dang de sbyin par bya'o snyam du dam bcas 
[\[196b.3\]]nas smon lam gyi phyir tshul khrims srung ste/_lag pa 'dod pa rnams la ni lag pa sbyin no/_/rkang pa 'dod pa rnams la ni rkang pa sbyin no/_/mig 'dod pa rnams la ni mig sbyin no/_/sha 'dod pa rnams la ni sha sbyin no/_/khrag 'dod pa rnams la ni khrag sbyin no/_/rkang 'dod pa rnams la ni rkang 
[\[196b.4\]]sbyin no/_/yan lag re re nas 'dod pa rnams la yang yan lag re re nas sbyin no/_/bran pho dang /_bran mo 'dod pa rnams la yang bran du 'gro bar bar bya na nor ram 'bru rnams yongs su btang ba lta ci smos te/_de ltar gyur cig ces de'i phyir tshul khrims srung ngo /_/gzhan yang 'jig rten 
[\[196b.5\]]dang 'thun pa'i bya ba du ma dag gis sems can gang dang gang la ci dang cis don du 'gyur ba de dang de la de dang de sbyin par bya'o/_/mi dga' ba med par yongs su gtang bar bya'o/_/phyis 'gyod pa med par sbyin par bya'o/_/rnam par smin pa la re ba med par sbyin par bya'o/_/sems can la 
[\[196b.6\]]phan gdags pa dang /_sems can la snying brtse ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rtag pa nyid bstan pa'i phyir sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa spyad de/_sems can thams cad la bsdus nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rtag pa nyid khong du chud par bya'o snyam mo/_/sprin chen snying po gzhan yang ting nge 
[\[196b.7\]]'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni sems can thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i rtag pa nyid yang dag par khong du chud par byed do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de 'di snyam du gzhan gyi don gyi phyir lag pa 'am/_rkang pa 'am/_bran pho dang /_bran mo yang rung ste/_sems 
[\[197a\]]
[\[197a.1\]]can gang dang gang la ci dang cis don du 'gyur ba de dang de sbyin par bya ste/_bdag gi lus don med par ma gyur par sems can rnams kyi longs spyod kyi rgyur gyur cig ces sems skyed par byed do/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das kyis mdo'i gzhung lugs gzhan dag las 
[\[197a.2\]]dge slong dag mi'i lus ni shag shig tu gyur pa lta bu rnag can gyi lus yin te/_rdza mkhan gyi phub ma mi gces pa bor bzhin du dgos pa'i don dag la don du 'gyur ba med do zhes bka' stsal na/_'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpas bdag gi lus don med par ma gyur cig ces lus 
[\[197a.3\]]yongs su gtong ngo zhes bgyi ba bcom ldan 'das kyis ci las dgongs te de skad gsungs/_bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po de ltar ma lta shig ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus ni chos kyi lus so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[197a.4\]]lus ni rus pa dang /_sha dang /_khrag 'dus pa ma yin te/_chos kyi dbyings yin pas na chos kyi lus yin gyi sha'i lus ma yin no zhes 'du shes bskyed par bya'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas ci las dgongs te/_chos kyi lus yin gyi sha'i lus ma 
[\[197a.5\]]yin no zhes de skad bstan pa mdzad/_gal te chos kyi lus lags na ni sems can rnams la yang dgos par mi 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i slad du zhe na/_gzhung lugs gzhan dag las bcom ldan 'das kyis nam mkha'i bya rjes ni shin tu bsgrims te btsal kyang rnyed par mi 'gyur ro zhes 
[\[197a.6\]]bka' stsal te/_de lta lags pas gal te 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus de 'dra ba zhig tu gyur na/_de lta bu'i mtshan nyid kyi chos kyi lus la ni sems can thams cad kyis kyang nam mkha'i bya'i rjes bzhin du gzhom pa 'am dbye bar mi nus so/_/bka' stsal pa/_sprin 
[\[197a.7\]]chen snying po de skad ma zer cig_/'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus sems can rnams la dgos pa de ni byin gyis brlabs pa'i lus yin par nges par rig par bya'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas ci las dgongs te byin gyis brlabs pa'i 
[\[197b\]]
[\[197b.1\]]lus yin no zhes gsung /_gal te byin gyis brlabs pa'i lus lags na ni sems can rnams la stsal ba na yang 'di ltar chos kyi lus su bzhag pas der 'gal ba chen por 'gyur ro/_/kye ma 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni sha'i lus sems can rnams la stsol bar bgyid do zhes 
[\[197b.2\]]gda' bas na sho be lags so zhes mchi bar 'gyur ro/_/bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po de skad ma zer cig 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus byin gyis brlabs pa gang yin pa de ni sgyu ma mkhan gyis sprul pa'i lus bzhin du blta bar bya'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[197b.3\]]de bzhin gshegs pas ci las dgongs te phyin ci log gi 'du shes dang /_phyin ci ma log pa'i 'du shes dang /_nyon mongs pa can gyi chos dang /_nyon mongs pa can ma yin pa'i chos zhes yang dang yang du de skad gsung /_bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po de ltar rnam pa gzhan du ma sems shig_/
[\[197b.4\]]'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus byin gyis brlabs pa gang yin pa de dag ni rtsi'i sdong bu lta bu'i lus yin no/_/'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni rtswa dang /_rtsig pa dang /_shing dang /_bong ba dang /_mig yor lta bur bsgom ste/_kye ma bdag gi lus ni 
[\[197b.5\]]spyod pa med cing byed pa med pa ste/_sems med pa can/_snying po med pa'i lus/_khams su ma gtogs pa ste/_ci ste 'di la la la zhig gcod par byed dam/_'byed par byed dam/_'phrog par byed dam/_rku bar byed kyang rung ste/_bdag gi lus rtsi'i sdong bu ltar don med par ma 
[\[197b.6\]]gyur cig ces sgom par byed do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos kyi lus su bzhag pa'i phyir ro/_/sprin chen snying po gzhan yang dper na rtsi'i sdong bu de'i rtsa ba khyer na yang 'di snyam du bdag gi rtsa ba khyer ro snyam du mi sems so/_/shun lpags sam/_pri'am/_lo ma'am/_me tog gam/_
[\[197b.7\]]'bras bu 'am/_mgo togs sam/_sdong bu 'am/_yal ga 'am/_bur ma 'am/_rtsa ba dag khyer yang rung ste/_de 'di snyam du bdag gi shun lpags khyer ro/_/bdag gi pri khyer ro/_/bdag gi lo ma khyer ro/_/snyam du mi sems so/_/de bzhin du 'di la gnas pa'i byang 
[\[198a\]]
[\[198a.1\]]chub sems dpa'i lus rtsi'i sdong bu las lag pa khyer ram/_rkang pa khyer ram/_mig khyer ram/_sha khyer ram/_khrag khyer ram/_rkang khyer ram/_yan lag re re nas khyer ram/_bran pho dang /_bran mo khyer ram/_nor ram 'bru khyer yang de 'di snyam du bdag gi 
[\[198a.2\]]lag pa khyer ro/_/bdag gi rkang pa khyer ro/_/bdag gi mig khyer ro/_/bdag gi sha khyer ro/_/bdag gi khrag khyer ro/_/bdag gi rkang khyer ro/_/bdag gi yan lag re re nas khyer ro/_/bdag gi bran pho dang /_bran mo khyer ro/_/bdag gi nor dang 'bru khyer ro snyam du mi sems 
[\[198a.3\]]te/_de ni sems can thams cad kyi 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi nad bsal ba'i phyir zhugs pa yin no/_/'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i lus ni phal pa tha ma'i lus ma yin gyi byin gyis brlabs pa'i lus dang /_bdud rtsi'i lus yin par rig par bya'o/_/
[\[198a.4\]]sprin chen snying po gzhan yang 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni gang gi tshe byin gyis brlabs pa'i lus zad par byed pa de'i tshe bya rgod dang /_chun lag la sogs pa srog chags rnams kyis de'i ro rtsi'i sdong bu bzhin du zas su za bar 'gyur la/_sems can de dag thams cad kyang ngan song 
[\[198a.5\]]du 'gro bar 'gyur ba de ni gnas med de/_lha dang mi'i nang du skye zhing sangs rgyas mang po mnyes par byed par 'gyur ro/_/sprin chen snying po gzhan yang ting nge 'dzin 'di myur du rtogs par 'dod pa'i dge slong ngam/_dge slong ma 'am/_dge bsnyen nam/_dge bsnyen mas ni 'di ltar sems bskyed 
[\[198a.6\]]par bya ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa ni rtag pa dang /_ther zug pa dang /_zhi ba dang /_brtan pa dang /_dam pa'i chos kyi mdo nub par mi 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir sems can gang dang gang la ci dang cis don du 'gyur ba de dang de la de dang de sbyin par bya'o snyam ste/_lag pa 'dod pa rnams la ni lag pa 
[\[198a.7\]]sbyin no/_/rkang pa 'dod pa rnams la ni rkang pa sbyin no/_/mig 'dod pa rnams la ni mig sbyin no/_/sha 'dod pa rnams la ni sha sbyin no/_/khrag 'dod pa rnams la ni khrag sbyin no/_/rkang 'dod pa rnams la ni rkang sbyin no/_/yan lag re re nas 'dod pa rnams la yang yan lag re re nas sbyin no/_/
[\[198b\]]
[\[198b.1\]]bran 'dod pa rnams la yang bran du 'gro bar bya'o/_/dam pa'i chos bstan pa dang bshad pa'i phyir yang bdag gi lag pa khyer bar gyur cig_/bdag gi rkang pa khyer bar gyur cig_/de bzhin du sna dang /_rna ba dang /_yan lag so so rnams dang /_de'i 'og tu bdag gi srog kyang yongs 
[\[198b.2\]]su gtong bar gyur cig ces sems bskyed par bya ste/_bdag nyid rtsi'i sdong bu bzhin du rtag tu bskyed par bya zhing ji srid 'tsho'i bar du yang sems bskyed par byas na se gol gtogs pa tsam du sems bskyed par bya ba lta ci smos/_sprin chen snying po gzhan yang dper na/_rta'i rgyal po rngog 
[\[198b.3\]]khyer dang ldan pa sprin gyi shugs can ni gru zhig pa'i mi rnams kyi phyir brtan pa'i sems kyis chas te/_ri ma la ya'i dbus su 'dug nas sgra chen po bsgrags te/_gang dag nga'i rgyab bam/_phongs sam/_bya la 'am/_tha na spu'i nyag ma tsam las 'ju ba'i sems can gang yin pa de dag thams cad 
[\[198b.4\]]bsgral bar bya'o/_/zhes zer ba de bzhin du ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni dge slong ngam/_dge slong ma 'am/_dge bsnyen nam/_dge bsnyen ma gang dag spyod pa can dang /_kun tu rgyu dang /_sems can gzhan dag la 'phya ba'i phyir ram/_bsting ba'i phyir ting nge 'dzin 'di 
[\[198b.5\]]las de bzhin gshegs pa rtag pa dang /_ther zug pa dang /_zhi ba dang /_brtan pa dang /_zab pa'i tshig gi don nam/_tshigs su bcad pa 'am/_tha na tshig gi phyed kyang rung ste/_rna lam du thos par byed pa dag dang /_gang dag 'chad pa yang rung ba'i sems can gru zhig par gyur pa dang 'dra 
[\[198b.6\]]ba de dag ngan song ngan 'gro log par ltung ba'i dra ba las grol bar byed do/_/sprin chen snying po gzhan yang ting nge 'dzin 'di rna lam du grag pa tsam gyis de dag mi'i dbang po'i phun sum tshogs pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/lha'i dbang po'i phun sum tshogs pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/
[\[198b.7\]]tshangs pa'i dbang po'i phun sum tshogs pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i phun sum tshogs pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/mdo 'di'i ting nge 'dzin gyi phun sum tshogs pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na ting nge 'dzin 'di la 
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' shin tu yang dag pa'i lta ba can lags/_bka' stsal pa/_ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag de kho na dang /_rtag pa dang /_bdag dang sems can du 'du shes pa yang dag par mthong ba 
[\[199a.2\]]dag ni shin tu yang dag pa'i lta ba can yin zhes nga smra'o/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas ci las dgongs te 'jig rten pa'i 'du shes [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa rnams rdzogs par bshad cing de skad bka' stsal/_bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po de kho na dang /_rtag pa dang /_
[\[199a.3\]]bdag dang /_sems can du 'du shes pa zhes bya ba ni thar pa'i don gyi tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rtag pa dang /_ther zug pa dang /_zhi ba dang /_brtan pa zhes bya ba ni dam pa'i chos nub par mi 'gyur ba'i tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[199a.4\]]kyis ci las dgongs te byis pa bzhin du de kho na dang /_rtag pa dang /_bdag dang /_sems can du 'du shes bskyed par bya'o zhes gsung /_bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po dar gyi srin bu bzhin du bdag nyid ma dkri shig_/ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[199a.5\]]chen po'i rang bzhin ni de yin no/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das kyis de snyed cig mdzad de/_zlos bu dang 'dren mar de ltar bka' stsal te ci bgyi/_bdag med pa'i chos nyid thugs su chud bzhin du lam drang po dang mi 'thun pa/_rtag pa dang /_bdag dang /_sems can du 'du shes 
[\[199a.6\]]ci'i slad du skyed du stsal/_bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong dag thar pa ni nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de de yang mtshan nyid med pa'o/_/gdags par bya ba'i thabs kyang med de/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyang de bzhin te nam 
[\[199a.7\]]mkha' dang mtshungs so zhes kyang bka' stsal/_de bzhin gshegs pa bye ba snyed kyang chu'i zla ba dang 'dra'o/_/sgyu ma dang 'dra'o/_/dngos po med pa'o/_/ya ma brla'o/_/rmi lam lta bu'i rang bzhin can no/_/chu shing gi sdong po ltar snying po med pa'o/_/glog dang sprin ltar mi brtan 
[\[199b\]]
[\[199b.1\]]pa'o/_/chos thams cad ni stong pa dang mtshan ma med pa'o/_/chos rnams kyi bya ba du ma dag ni nam mkha'i rlung gi rjes kyi rang bzhin can no zhes kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal/_yang 'jig rten na sems can thams cad ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i chos su yang dag par 'du'o zhes kyang 
[\[199b.2\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal na/_bcom ldan 'das mi rtag pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin thugs su chud pa bzhin du rtag pa dang /_bdag dang /_sems can du 'du shes ji ltar skyed du stsal/_rtag pa'i spyod pa yang ji ltar bsgom par bgyi/_spyod pa 'di'i rjes su yang ji ltar bslab par 
[\[199b.3\]]bgyi/_yang bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag sangs rgyas thams cad kyis shin tu bsgrims te/_'dus byas kyi chos thams cad khongs su gtogs pa'i nang du stsal kyang /_bdag tu 'du shes pa ma rnyed do zhes bdag med pa dang /_srog med pa dang /_gang zag 
[\[199b.4\]]med par bka' stsal te/_'gro ba 'di dag thams cad stong par yang dag par gsungs na rtag pa dang /_bdag dang /_sems can du 'du shes zhes bka' stsal pa 'di la don ci zhig mchis/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po khyod gar 'bros/_khyod gang du 'phyan/_
[\[199b.5\]]da phyin cad de skad ma zer cig_/ting nge 'dzin 'di la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni chos thams cad mi 'thun no zhes skrag par mi byed la/_chos thams cad mi 'thun pa med pa'i phyir yang tshul khrims rgya cher mi srung zhing sems kyang sgom par mi byed de/_chos thams cad mi 'thun pa med 
[\[199b.6\]]pas rab tu dga' nas byang chub sems dpa'i bya ba rtsom pa las nur bar mi byed do/_/chos thams cad chad do zhes kyang skrag par mi byed la/_chos thams cad chad pa med pa'i phyir yang tshul khrims mi srung zhing sems kyang sgom par mi byed do/_/chos thams cad chad pa med pas rab tu

[\[213a.1\]]ba'i rang bzhin can du skye bar 'gyur te/_de'i tshe de'i dus na bu mo de ji tsam du skyes pa de tsam du bdag nyid kyi rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor 'phel bar byed de/_rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor de 'byor cing rgyas par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du mun pa can gyi yul 'khor yang de'i tshe 'byor cing rgyas par 'gyur te skye bo mang pos kyang gang bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[213a.2\]]'bru rnams kyi lo tog kyang shin tu phun sum tshogs par 'gyur ro/_/mi rnams kyang bden par smra bar 'gyur ro/_/nad med pa dang /_'jigs pa med pa dang /_bde bar yang 'gyur ro/_/de nas bu mo'i pha rgyal po dpal 'phel gyis bdag nyid kyi bsod nams kyis grong khyer dang /_yul 'khor 'byor cing rgyas la yul gyi mi rnams shin 
[\[213a.3\]]tu legs par 'khod pa dang /_'bru shin tu tshogs par mthong nas bdag nyid kyi bu mo'i ming lo tog 'byor ma zhes yon tan dang 'thun pa'i ming 'dogs so/_/de nas rim gyis rgyal po dpal 'phel rang gi bsod nams zad nas 'chi ba'i dus byed par 'gyur ro/_/de nas grong khyer dang /_grong rdal dang /_yul gyi mi rnams 'dus te/_
[\[213a.4\]]gzhon nu rgyal po nus pa 'ga' yang med kyis lha mo lo tog 'byor ma 'di nyid rgyal por dbang bskur la rgyal po'i pho brang du de 'dra ba'i gzhon nu 'ga' zhig ming khyer tsam du bzhag ste/_de ltar byas na legs so snyam mo/_/de nas lha mo de dang /_rgyal bu gzhon nu de rgyal po byed du bcug ste/_pho 'ud ti mo 'ud ti bzhin du snyad 
[\[213a.5\]]btags nas bdag nyid kyi yul du bdag nyid kyis bud med kyi gzugs kyis rgyal srid chen po yongs su spyad de/_mu stegs can ngan pa'i sde mngon par 'dul bar byed cing 'dzam bu'i gling gi sa gzhi thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten gyi mngon mtshan can du byas nas bkur stir byed/_bla mar byed/_rim gror byed/_mchod 
[\[213a.6\]]par byed de/_rin po che thams cad kyi gdugs chen po dang /_rin po che thams cad kyi ba dan rnams dang /_rin po che thams cad kyi rnam par rgyal ba'i ba dan rnams dang /_spos thams cad kyis bsgos pa'i tsan dan gyi phye ma dang /_spos dang /_me tog thams cad dang /_rnga pa Ta ha dang /_khar rnga dang /_rdza rnga dang /_pi bang dang /_gling 
[\[213a.7\]]bu dang /_sil snyan brgya phrag thams cad kyis mchod pa byas nas 'dzam bu'i gling thams cad bskor te/_dge slong tshul khrims dang ldan pa dang /_yon tan dang ldan pa rnams la chos gos dang /_bsod snyoms dang mal stan dang /_na ba'i gsos sman dang /_yo byad rnams dang ldan par byas te/_pha rol tu phyin pa bcu'i spyad pa spyod cing rtag tu 
[\[213b\]]
[\[213b.1\]]bslab pa'i gzhi lnga bzung nas dbul po la sogs pa khyim bkol gyi sems can thams cad gang dang gang la ci dang cis don du 'gyur ba de dang de la de dang de byin nas 'du shes bcu'i bsdu bas bsdus te spyod par byed cing /_sems can bye ba phrag mang po dag rim bzhin du btul te sems can de dag gi dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smin par 
[\[213b.2\]]shes nas/_sprin chen snying po'i mdo 'di la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis gsungs pa mtshungs pa med pa'i mdo rin po che'i dam pa'i chos bstan pa 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir ci'i thabs kyis kyang mtshungs pa med pa'i sgra sgrogs par byed cing /_mtshungs pa med pa'i glegs bam bris pa'i snod sangs rgyas kyis gang dang 
[\[213b.3\]]gang du byin gyis brlabs pa'i gnas rnams su 'jog par byed de/_de ltar mtshungs pa med pa'i sa bon bskyed nas de'i 'og tu 'chi ba'i dus byed par 'gyur ro/_/de'i tshe lha mo lo tog 'byor ma shes rab chen mo thabs mkhas pa zab mo shes pa dang ldan pa'i lha mos bud med kyi lus las ldog par 'gyur ro/_/sprin chen snying 
[\[213b.4\]]pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ci lha mo 'di sems can thams cad kyi dad pa bskyed pa'i slad du cho 'phrul chen po bgyis nas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur ram/_bka' stsal pa/_sprin chen snying po ci lha mo 'di cho 'phrul chen po byed mi nus zhes khyod smra'am/_bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lags so/_/bka' stsal pa/_
[\[213b.5\]]sprin chen snying po lha mo 'di cho 'phrul chen po ston mi nus so zhes de skad ma zer cig_/'on kyang 'dul ba'i dbang gis ni mi 'dod do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dzam bu'i gling 'di na lha mo 'di la sogs pa dge slong ngam/_dge slong ma tshul khrims dang ldan par gyur nas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar mi 'gyur ba'i sems can 
[\[213b.6\]]de gang yang med de de ni gnas med do/_/sprin chen snying po de bas na ma 'ongs pa'i sems can bsod nams chung ngu thams cad kyi dad pa bskyed pa'i phyir mdo 'di las byang chub sems dpa' rnams lung bstan par byed do zhes nga smra'o/_/sprin chen snying po de'i tshe lha mo 'di bud med kyi lus las bzlog nas de bzhin 
[\[213b.7\]]gshegs pa 'od dpag med kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'jig rten gyi khams bde ba can du skye ba len par 'gyur te/_'jig rten gyi khams der bde ba nyams su myong nas rim gyis sangs rgyas su 'gyur ro/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das thabs la mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i lha mo 'di yun ji srid cig na sangs rgyas su 
[\[214a\]]
[\[214a.1\]]'gyur 'di'i ming yang ji skad ces bgyi bar 'gyur/_bcom ldan 'das de'i 'jig rten gyi khams ni ci 'dra bar 'gyur/_bka' stsal pa/_lha mo 'di ni bskal pa grangs med pa phrag lnga brgya 'das pa na stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
[\[214a.2\]]mngon par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas/_rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa/_bde bar gshegs pa/_'jig rten mkhyen pa/_skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba bla na med pa/_lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gtsang zhing bden 
[\[214a.3\]]pa'i mtshams 'phel bar mdzad pa zhes bya bar 'gyur ro/_/'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed 'di yang 'jig rten gyi khams gtsang ma sbyong ba zhes bya bar 'gyur ro/_/bskal pa ni gtsang zhing bden pa'i mtshams zhes bya bar 'gyur te/_'jig rten gyi khams gtsang ma sbyong ba de 'byor par 'gyur/_rgyas par 'gyur/_bde ba dang /_
[\[214a.4\]]nyams dga' ba dang /_lo legs pa dang /_skye bo dang /_mi mang pos gang bar 'gyur ro/_/'byor cing rgyas par gyur pa de'i tshe 'dzam bu'i gling 'dir grong khyer gtsang ma bsung ldan zhes bya ba zhig 'byung bar 'gyur te/_grong khyer gtsang ma bsung ldan du mi bye ba brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa brgyad 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/dpe 
[\[214a.5\]]med pa'i grong khyer der ra ba dang /_yang tog dang /_bla 'tshogs dang /_'obs dpe med pa dag kyang 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/dag cing nyon mongs pa zhi bar byed pa'i chu'i klung nag po bzang zhes bya ba ni de'i tshe gtsang ma bzang po zhes bya bar 'gyur te/_'gram pha rol tshu rol dpe med pa'i shing ljon pa'i kun dga' ra ba nyams 
[\[214a.6\]]dga' ba dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/'jig rten gyi khams gtsang ma sbyong ba de yang 'di lta ste dper na dga' ldan gyi lha rnams kyi longs spyod dang /_yongs su spyod pa bzhin te/_ri dang /_rdo dang /_bong ba dang /_gyo mo dang /_gseg ma la sogs pa med par 'gyur zhing mu stegs can gyi ming yang med par 'gyur ro/_/nyan thos dang /_rang 
[\[214a.7\]]sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ming yang mi grag par 'gyur ro/_/skyes pa dang bud med dag gi byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun yang 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de na nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas shes pa'i gtam gyi ming yang med de/_theg pa chen po'i mdo'i gtam rgyas par ston par 'gyur ro/_/bdud dang phyir rgol ba bcom pa'i 
[\[214b\]]
[\[214b.1\]]sems can rnams 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/dag cing bzod pa chen po'i rnyed pa thob pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/sa gzhi yang nor bu rin po che thams cad las grub par 'gyur te/_'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser gyi me tog dang /_pad ma thams cad kyis spros par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de yang tshe dpag tu 
[\[214b.2\]]med par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du mi rnams kyi tshe yang tshad med par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de ni grong khyer gtsang ma bsung ldan du bzhugs par 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das de'i smon lam gyi khyad par gyis na ma 'ongs pa'i sems can gyi tshogs mang po dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa de'i mtshan thos pa 
[\[214b.3\]]tsam gyis rang gi lus las shi 'phos ma thag tu 'jig rten gyi khams gtsang ma sbyong ba der grong khyer gtsang ma bsung ldan du skye bar 'gyur zhes nga smra'o/_/ngan song bzhi yang de dag gi spyod yul gyi sar mi 'gyur ro/_/sprin chen snying po de bas na nga ni bcom ldan 'das de'i 'jig rten gyi khams gtsang ma sbyong ba dang /_
[\[214b.4\]]bskal pa gtsang zhing bden pa'i mtshams zhes bya ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa gtsang zhing bden pa'i mtshams 'phel bar mdzad pa zhes bya ba yon tan gzung ba smra'o/_/de nas 'khor chen po'i nang nas lha dang mi'i tshogs tshad med pas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa chen po'i las byas te gar byas/_glu blangs 
[\[214b.5\]]nas bcom ldan 'das de'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la sems bskyed do/_/'di ni ma 'ongs pa'i sems can bye ma snyed dag lung ston pa sprin chen po'i mdo tsheg bar brgyad par sbyar ba'i le'u 'bum pa las lung bstan pa'i rgyud yin no/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/_sprin chen
